exempt pdfcer-core method pdfcer_core::parser::Parser::duplicate_keys -- every duplicate key one `Parser` resolved. This shell never constructs a `Parser` and never will: it opens documents through `Document::load`, which drives the parser internally, tags each record with its object and hands the result out as `Document::load_anomalies()` -- the call `app::status::anomalies` actually makes. Reading the parser's own list would require standing up a second parse of the same bytes, and would produce a report that disagreed with the one on screen the moment the two parses were given different `LoadOptions`.

exempt pdfcer-core struct pdfcer_core::parser::DuplicateKeyRecord -- the parser's own untagged record of one duplicate-key decision (key, kept, discarded). This shell wants all three facts and already has them, one layer up and better: `document::LoadAnomaly::DuplicateDictKey` carries the same three PLUS the `ObjId` they came from, and `app::status::anomalies` reads it live at draw time to say which object contradicted itself. The record cannot answer that -- the engine tags it with the object as it carries it out of the parser -- so a shell that reached for `DuplicateKeyRecord` would be trading down to the version with the object number missing. exempt pdfcer-core struct pdfcer_core::xref::HybridPartition -- xref-internal plumbing that is public only because `pub mod xref;` publishes the whole module. It exists to answer ONE question, and the writer is the only thing that asks it: `is_reproducible()` gates `writer::save`'s full rewrite, and `Document`'s own field holding it is PRIVATE, read at writer/save.rs and nowhere else. The single public accessor `Document::hybrid_partition()` hands out a reference to it, so this shell COULD hold one -- and would learn nothing an operator can act on, because the only outcome that reaches him is already carried by `WriteError::HybridFullRewrite`, which this shell renders today (O157). Its two fields are a set of hidden object numbers and a did-the-XRefStm-parse flag: file-structure facts about SS 7.5.8.4, not document facts. DELETE THIS LINE if a surface ever needs to say WHICH objects a hybrid file hides -- that is the one thing in here the refusal variant does not carry.

exempt pdfcer-core variant pdfcer_core::page_tree::PageTreeError::BadResources -- a NEW refusal from engine `Pass 290.0` (2026-09-10): `/Resources` is present and is not a dictionary. The widening in the same Pass is what this shell consumes -- an ABSENT `/Resources`, on the page and every ancestor, now resolves to the empty dictionary Table 30 names, and the shell discloses that through `Diagnostics::page_resources_defaulted` and `TextDiagnostics::pages_resources_defaulted`, both wired the same day. The refusal half is exempted because this shell never NAMES a `PageTreeError`: the only page-tree failure it can hold is the `String` in `StampCollection::page_tree_error`, which it prints verbatim through `text::stamps::properties_page_tree_unreadable` precisely so a cycle in `/Kids` and a bad `/Resources` stay distinguishable to the operator without the shell having an opinion about either. There is no act this shell would take for a bad `/Resources` that differs from any other page-tree refusal -- it cannot repair the file. DELETE THIS LINE the day a structure-repair surface exists, because that surface would need the variant, not the sentence.
exempt pdfcer-core variant pdfcer_core::vector::edit::VectorEditError::DuplicateObjectInMove -- the panel builds its move list from a selection, which holds each object once, so it never names an index twice; were it to, the funnel shows the engine's own Display sentence, and matching the variant would only re-word it.
exempt pdfcer-core variant pdfcer_core::vector::edit::VectorEditError::EmptyTextRunMove -- `move_text_runs` handed no runs. Unreachable from here by construction: `canvas::moving::run_move` returns before building the move when no line is selected, so there is no sentence for the operator to read.
exempt pdfcer-core variant pdfcer_core::vector::edit::VectorEditError::TransformInsideTextObject -- a transform asked to act inside a text object. The shell transforms whole objects only; this reaches the operator through `EditRefusal`'s generic arm if the engine ever answers it, and it names no act the operator can take differently.
exempt pdfcer-print const pdfcer_print::imposition::POSTER_MARK_GAP_PT -- the gap the engine leaves between a poster tile's image and its cut marks. The shell draws the marks from the tile's own `marks` and `label_rect`, which already include it; restating the gap here would be a second copy of the geometry.
exempt pdfcer-render field pdfcer_render::font::RenderPolicy::omit_annotations -- the interpreter's derived mirror of RenderOptions::omit_annotations, which this shell sets through `with_omit_annotations` in `canvas::dimpreview::underlay`; it constructs no RenderPolicy. Per field, as for stroke_display.
exempt pdfcer-render field pdfcer_render::font::RenderPolicy::stroke_display -- RenderPolicy is the interpreter's INTERNAL derived bundle, not a shell-facing type: every one of its fields' own doc comments reads "See RenderOptions::<the same name>", and the engine builds a RenderPolicy from the RenderOptions a caller hands it. This shell sets RenderOptions::stroke_display (View > Display > Line weights, O137) and constructs no RenderPolicy anywhere in the workspace. Exempted PER FIELD rather than wildcarded for the whole type, deliberately: every future RenderPolicy field mirrors a RenderOptions field this shell probably DOES want, so each one should cost one line and one look.
exempt pdfcer-render field pdfcer_render::interpret::Diagnostics::strokes_width_fixed -- how many strokes a render drew at the fixed print width. The width is the operator's own setting on the Print dialog's Pages tab and the caption under it states it; a count of strokes that obeyed it says nothing he can act on.
exempt pdfcer-render method pdfcer_render::text::TextState::clips -- whether the current text rendering mode (Tr 4-7) adds glyphs to the clip. The rasteriser consumes it while painting; the shell holds no TextState and draws only what the renderer returns.
exempt pdfcer-render method pdfcer_render::text::TextState::text_space_params -- the interpreter's §9.4.4 text-space matrix, shared by its glyph placement and the Type 3 glyph CTM. This shell never interprets a text state; it reads glyph boxes from extraction and pixels from the renderer.
exempt pdfcer-render variant pdfcer_render::font::subset::SubsetError::Cff2NotSupported -- a refusal this shell words through the engine's own Display text: `app::actions::textstyle::donor_plan` and `handsign::typed` both map every `plan_subset` error with `to_string()`, so no variant is matched here and none needs a sentence of ours.
exempt pdfcer-render variant pdfcer_render::mesh::Shade::Spot -- a mesh vertex colour inside the rasterizer: the shading-mesh interpreter builds and interpolates Shade values and composites them; this shell holds no mesh, constructs no Shade, and sees the result only as rendered pixels.
